The plate follows.</div>\n      </div>\n    </aside>\n  </div>\n</section>\n\n<!-- ============ ESSAYS ============ -->\n<section class=\"essays\" id=\"essays\" aria-label=\"Essays and ideas\">\n  <div class=\"wrap\">\n    <div class=\"zone-head zh rv\"><span class=\"zh-no\">05</span><div class=\"zh-x\"><h2>Essays &amp; Ideas</h2><span class=\"more\">Argument, criticism, framework</span></div></div>\n    <div class=\"es-grid rv\">\n      <div>\n        <a class=\"es-lead\" href=\"/editorial/machinic-taste\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">\n          <span class=\"meta\"><span class=\"folio\">143</span> · Ruby Justice Thelot · June 29, 2026</span>\n          <h3>Machinic Taste</h3></a>\n        <div class=\"es-body\">\n          <p>As AI-generated images get increasingly weirder, we are gaining a premier view into a new form of taste, something post-human, the first expressions of machinic taste. Accelerationist thinker Nick Land asserted, &ldquo;Nothing human makes it out of the near future.&rdquo; Could we be seeing the first inklings that nothing human makes it out of digital culture?</p>\n          <p>Slop is &ldquo;digital content of low quality that is produced usually in quantity by means of artificial intelligence.&rdquo; But what we deem low quality is in fact the result of a very specific reward function. There is something that wants these images; they are not gratuitous.</p>\n          <a class=\"es-more\" href=\"/editorial/machinic-taste\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">Keep reading →</a>\n class=\"es-t\">Neural Unconscious</span><span class=\"es-m\">Peter Bauman · 2025</span></a><a class=\"es-item\" href=\"/editorial/can-art-replace-religion\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"es-no\">055</span><span class=\"es-t\">Can Art Replace Religion?</span><span class=\"es-m\">Peter Bauman · 2024</span></a><a class=\"es-item\" href=\"/editorial/seams-and-synthesis-schizocollage-and-ai-aesthetics\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"es-no\">122</span><span class=\"es-t\">Seams and Synthesis: Schizocollage and AI Aesthetics</span><span class=\"es-m\">Brian Droitcour · 2026</span></a><a class=\"es-item\" href=\"/editorial/post-human--ai-art\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"es-no\">093</span><span class=\"es-t\">Post(human)-AI Art</span><span class=\"es-m\">Peter Bauman · 2025</span></a><a class=\"es-item\" href=\"/editorial/a-mean-defense\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"es-no\">083</span><span class=\"es-t\">A Mean Defense</span><span class=\"es-m\">Peter Bauman · 2025</span></a></div>\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"fw rv\"><div class=\"fw-head\"><span class=\"lbl\" style=\"color:var(--orange)\">The Framework</span><h3 class=\"fw-title\">Demystifying Generative Art</h3><p class=\"fw-intro\">Four parts, published between July 2023 and December 2024, building a way to look at generative art.</p></div><div class=\"fw-tabs\" role=\"tablist\"><button class=\"fw-tab on\" data-i=\"0\" role=\"tab\" aria-selected=\"true\"><span class=\"fw-rom\">I</span><span class=\"fw-nm\">Art</span></button><button class=\"fw-tab\" data-i=\"1\" role=\"tab\" aria-selected=\"false\"><span class=\"fw-rom\">II</span><span class=\"fw-nm\">Aesthetics</span></button><button class=\"fw-tab\" data-i=\"2\" role=\"tab\" aria-selected=\"false\"><span class=\"fw-rom\">III</span><span class=\"fw-nm\">Systems</span></button><button class=\"fw-tab\" data-i=\"3\" role=\"tab\" aria-selected=\"false\"><span class=\"fw-rom\">IV</span><span class=\"fw-nm\">Autonomy</span></button></div><div class=\"fw-panels\"><div class=\"fw-panel on\" data-i=\"0\" role=\"tabpanel\"><p class=\"fw-body\">Peter Bauman (Monk Antony) develops a framework for appreciating and analyzing generative art, art using autonomous systems in the vein of Sol LeWitt or Yoko Ono, through an investigation of the movement’s locus of artistic intent.</p><a class=\"fw-read\" href=\"/editorial/demystifying-generative-art\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"fw-folio\">005</span>July 20, 2023 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Read part I →</a></div><div class=\"fw-panel\" data-i=\"1\" role=\"tabpanel\"><p class=\"fw-body\">A closer look at one of the five framework components, Results, the outputs of a generative system. With Tyler Hobbs, Kim Asendorf, Andreas Gysin, Leander Herzog, Erick Calderon, William Mapan, Lauren Lee McCarthy, Sougwen Chung, Christiane Paul, Patricio González Vivo, Linda Dounia and Golan Levin.</p><a class=\"fw-read\" href=\"/editorial/demystifying-generative-aesthetics\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"fw-folio\">037</span>March 21, 2024 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Read part II →</a></div><div class=\"fw-panel\" data-i=\"2\" role=\"tabpanel\"><p class=\"fw-body\">Bauman investigates another layer of the Framework, systems themselves. He spoke to Frieder Nake, Casey Reas, Emily Xie, DEAFBEEF, Matt DesLauriers, Jen Lowe and Patricio González Vivo for the piece.</p><a class=\"fw-read\" href=\"/editorial/demystifying-generative-systems\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"fw-folio\">049</span>June 20, 2024 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Read part III →</a></div><div class=\"fw-panel\" data-i=\"3\" role=\"tabpanel\"><p class=\"fw-body\">Bauman investigates another framework component, autonomy, control within generative systems. The essay includes discussions with Christiane Paul, Matt DesLauriers, Sougwen Chung and Zach Lieberman.</p><a class=\"fw-read\" href=\"/editorial/demystifying-generative-autonomy\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span class=\"fw-folio\">070</span>December 12, 2024 &nbsp;·&nbsp; href=\"https://timeline.lerandom.art\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><div class=\"tl-left\"><span class=\"lbl\">Timeline · Moment of the day</span><span class=\"tl-year\" id=\"tl-year\">1960</span><span class=\"tl-meta\">One of hundreds of milestones across ten chapters, from 70,000 years ago to now, edited by Peter Bauman.</span></div><div class=\"tl-right\"><h3 class=\"tl-title\" id=\"tl-title\">Kurd Alsleben and Cord Passow make analog computer drawings</h3><p class=\"tl-body\" id=\"tl-body\">Artist Kurd Alsleben in collaboration with physicist Cord Passow used an analogue computer (EAI 231 R) in Hamburg to create parametric generative art. The analog computer, combined with a mechanical drawing mechanism, produced graphical works of waves and lines based on the changing results of an equation.</p><span class=\"tl-cta\">Explore the Timeline →</span></div></a>\n</section>\n\n<!-- ============ REGISTER ============ -->\n<section class=\"wrap reg\" id=\"register\" aria-label=\"The archive\">\n  <div class=\"reg-top rv '#pod=' + t[1]\n           : '#subject=' + encodeURIComponent(t[1]);\n    }\n    var armed = {}, cyc = {}, blockIdx = 0, lastBlock = null;\n    var walker = document.createTreeWalker(body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, { acceptNode: function(n){\n      if (!n.nodeValue || n.nodeValue.length < 3) return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;\n      for (var el = n.parentElement; el && el !== body; el = el.parentElement){\n        var tg = el.tagName;\n        if (tg === 'A' || tg === 'SCRIPT' || tg === 'STYLE' || tg === 'BUTTON') return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;\n        if (el.classList && el.classList.contains('lbl')) return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;\n      }\n      return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT;\n    }});\n    var nodes = []; blockIdx++; }\n      var txt = node.nodeValue, at = 0, m, hits = [];\n      while ((m = rx.exec(txt.slice(at)))){\n        var form = m[1], rec = byForm[form], key = rec[1], ent = SL[key];\n        var s = at + m.index, e = s + form.length;\n        at = e;\n        var okB = s === 0 || /[^A-Za-z0-9\\u00C0-\\u024F]/.test(txt[s - 1]);\n        var okA = e === txt.length || /[^A-Za-z0-9\\u00C0-\\u024F]/.test(txt[e]);\n        if (!okB || !okA) continue;\n        if (rec[2]){\n          if (/[A-Z][\\w'\\u2019-]*\\s+$/.test(txt.slice(0, s))) continue;\n          if (/^\\s+[A-Z][a-z]/.test(txt.slice(e))) continue;\n        }\n        if (armed[form] != null && blockIdx < armed[form]) continue;\n        var ts = ent[2].filter(function(t){ return !(t[0] === 'p' && t[1] === ep); });\n        if (!ts.length) continue;\n        cyc[key] = cyc[key] || 0;\n        var tgt = ts[cyc[key] % ts.length]; cyc[key]++;\n        armed[form] = tgt[0] === 's' ? 1e9 : blockIdx + 3;\n        hits.push([s, e, hrefFor(tgt), ent[0]]);\n      }\n      if (!hits.length) return;\n      var frag = document.createDocumentFragment(), pos = 0;\n      hits.forEach(function(h){\n        if (h[0] > pos) frag.appendChild(document.createTextNode(txt.slice(pos, h[0])));\n        var a = document.createElement('a');\n        a.className = 'lr-sl'; 'tez' : null;\n    if (!kind) { status('That doesn\\u2019t look like an Ethereum (0x\\u2026) or Tezos (tz\\u2026) address.'); return; }\n    status('Reading the chain\\u2026');\n    loadData().then(function(){\n      return kind === 'tez' ? hitTez(a) : hitEth(a.toLowerCase());\n    }).then(function(found){\n      var keys = Object.keys(found);\n      if (!keys.length) { status('No artists from the archive recognized in this wallet \\u2014 the map covers our featured artists and grows weekly.'); return; }\n      keys.sort(function(x, y){ return (SL[x] ? SL[x][0] : x) < (SL[y] ? SL[y][0] : y) ? -1 : 1; });\n      out.innerHTML = '<div class=\"wal-status\">The archive recognizes ' + keys.length + (keys.length === 1 ? ' artist' : ' artists') + ' in this collection.</div>'\n        + keys.map(function(k){\n          var ent = SL[k]; if (!ent) return '';\n          var links = ent[2].slice(0, 3).map(function(t){\n            var href = t[0] === 'a' ? '/editorial/' + t[1] : t[0] === 'p' ? '#pod=' + t[1] : '#subject=' + encodeURIComponent(t[1]);\n            var lbl = t[0] === 'a' ? 'Read the editorial' : t[0] === 'p' ? 'Episode ' + ('0' + t[1]).slice(-2) : 'In the subject index';\n            return '<a href=\"' + href + '\">' + lbl + '</a>';\n          }).join('');\n          links += '<a href=\"#subject=' + encodeURIComponent(k) + '\">All appearances</a>';\n          return '<div class=\"wal-artist\"><h3>' + esc(ent[0]) + '</h3><div class=\"wal-links\">' + links + '</div></div>';\n        }).join('');\n    }).catch(function(){ status('The chain read failed \\u2014 try again in a moment.'); });\n  });\n})();\n\n/* ---------- EXPERIMENT: Near in the space — remove with its CSS and similar.json ---------- */\n(function(){\n  var body = document.getElementById('ent-body'); if (!body) return;\n  var SIM = null;\n  function load(cb){\n    if (SIM) { cb(); return; }\n    fetch('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/monkantony/lr-media/main/similar.json')\n      .then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(d){ SIM = d; cb(); }).catch(function(){});\n  }\n  function keyOf(){\n    var h = /#subject=([^&]+)/.exec(location.hash || '');\n    if (h) return decodeURIComponent(h[1]);\n    var rt = (document.getElementById('ent-route') || {}).textContent || '';\n    var m = rt.match(/subjects?\\/(.+)$/);\n    return m ? m[1].replace(/-/g, ' ') : null;\n  }\n  function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;'); }\n  function strip(k){\n    var old = document.getElementById('ent-sim'); if (old) old.remove();\n    var near = SIM[k]; if (!near || !near.length) return;\n    var el = document.createElement('div');\n    el.id = 'ent-sim';\n    el.innerHTML = '<div class=\"sim-head\">Near in the space</div><div class=\"sim-row\">'\n      + near.map(function(n){\n          return '<a href=\"#subject=' + encodeURIComponent(n[0]) + '\">' + esc(n[1]) + '</a>';\n        }).join('') + '</div>';\n    body.parentNode.insertBefore(el, body);\n  }\n  new MutationObserver(function(){\n    var k = keyOf(); if (!k) return;\n    load(function(){ strip(decodeURIComponent(k)); });\n  }).observe(body, { childList: true });\n})();\n\n/* ---------- perf: videos decode only while on screen ---------- */\n(function(){\n  if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;\n  var vids = document.querySelectorAll('#lrw video');\n  if (!vids.length) return;\n  var alive = false;\n  var io = new IntersectionObserver(function(es){\n    alive = true;\n    es.forEach(function(e){\n      var v = e.target;\n      if (e.isIntersecting) {\n        if (v.dataset.lrWas === '1' || (v.autoplay && v.paused)) { delete v.dataset.lrWas; v.play().catch(function(){}); }\n      } else if (!v.paused) { v.dataset.lrWas = '1'; v.pause(); }\n    });\n  }, { rootMargin: '200px' });\n  [].forEach.call(vids, function(v){ io.observe(v); });\n  /* if the observer pipeline is stalled (loaded machine), give up managing and just play */\n  setTimeout(function(){\n    if (alive) return;\n    io.disconnect();\n    [].forEach.call(vids, function(v){ if (v.autoplay && v.paused) v.play().catch(function(){}); });\n  }, 3000);\n})();\n\n/* ---------- Le Random Radio: the archive on air, idents from deep time between pieces ---------- */\n(function(){\n  var root = document.getElementById('radio'); if (!root) return;\n  var RAWM = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/monkantony/lr-media/main/';\n  var Q = null, IDENTS = null, ARTS = null, idx = 0, playingIdent = false;\n  var a = new Audio(); a.preload = 'none';\n  function mulberry(x){ return function(){ x |= 0; x = x + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;\n    var t = Math.imul(x ^ x >>> 15, 1 | x); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;\n    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }\n  function boot(cb){\n    if (Q) { cb(); return; }\n    Promise.all([\n      fetch(RAWM + 'footer_data.txt').then(function(r){ return r.text(); }),\n      fetch(RAWM + 'idents/index.json').then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).catch(function(){ return {}; })\n    ]).then(function(rs){\n      var d = JSON.parse(rs[0].slice(rs[0].indexOf('{')));\n      ARTS = d.arts;\n      var seed = (window.__LRW_ED ? window.__LRW_ED.seed : 42), rnd = mulberry(seed);\n      Q = Object.keys(d.foot).filter(function(s){ return d.foot[s].au; })\n        .map(function(s){ return { s: s, u: d.foot[s].au[0] }; });\n      (window.__lrPodRoll || []).forEach(function(e){\n        if (e.u) Q.push({ ep: e.n, u: e.u, t: e.t });\n      });\n      for (var i = Q.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { var k = Math.floor(rnd() * (i + 1)); var tmp = Q[i]; Q[i] = Q[k]; Q[k] = tmp; }\n      IDENTS = Object.keys(rs[1]).map(function(id){ return { u: RAWM + 'idents/' + id + '.mp3', t: rs[1][id] }; });\n      cb();\n    }).catch(function(){});\n  }\n  function setTitle(t){ document.getElementById('radio-t').textContent = t; }\n  function playCurrent(){\n    var tr = Q[idx % Q.length];\n    a.src = tr.u; a.play().catch(function(){});\n    if (tr.ep != null) {\n      setTitle(tr.t);\n      document.getElementById('radio-lbl').textContent = 'The podcast \\u00b7 Episode ' + ('0' + tr.ep).slice(-2);\n    } else {\n      var r = ARTS[tr.s];\n      setTitle(r ? r[2] : tr.s);\n      document.getElementById('radio-lbl').textContent = 'Now playing \\u00b7 \\u2116' + (r ? String(r[0]).padStart(3, '0') : '');\n    }\n  }\n  function next(){\n    idx++;\n    if (IDENTS.length && idx % 3 === 0) {          /* every third slot: a moment from deep time */\n      playingIdent = true;\n      var id = IDENTS[Math.floor(Math.random() * IDENTS.length)];\n      a.src = id.u; a.play().catch(function(){});\n      document.getElementById('radio-lbl').textContent = 'From the timeline';\n      setTitle(id.t);\n    } else { playingIdent = false; playCurrent(); }\n  }\n  a.addEventListener('ended', function(){ if (playingIdent) { playingIdent = false; playCurrent(); } else next(); });\n  var pl = root.querySelector('.r-ic-play'), pa = root.querySelector('.r-ic-pause');\n  a.addEventListener('play', function(){ pl.style.display = 'none'; pa.style.display = 'block'; root.classList.add('on'); });\n  a.addEventListener('pause', function(){ pl.style.display = 'block'; pa.style.display = 'none'; root.classList.remove('on'); });\n  document.getElementById('radio-toggle').addEventListener('click', function(){\n    var p = document.getElementById('radio-panel');\n    p.style.display = p.style.display === 'none' ? '' : 'none';\n  });\n  document.getElementById('radio-play').addEventListener('click', function(){\n    boot(function(){\n      if (a.paused) { if (!a.src) playCurrent(); else a.play().catch(function(){}); }\n      else a.pause();\n    });\n  });\n  document.getElementById('radio-skip').addEventListener('click', function(){ boot(next); });\n})();\n"}
